- ddraw desc/private-data guest-pointer iteration: `IDirectDrawSurface7::SetPrivateData`/`GetPrivateData`/`FreePrivateData` and the `IDirectDraw7`/`IDirectDraw` `EnumDisplayModes` filter-desc plus `GetDisplayMode` reads all sliced `ctx.memory[ptr..]` or did `Memory::read`/`write` on pointers that passed only a `!= 0` check, so an out-of-range non-null guest pointer panicked the host. Each entry now validates `guidTag`/`lpData`/`cbSize`, the `lpcbBufferSize` size read/write, the `lpBuffer` copy, and both `DDSURFACEDESC`/`DDSURFACEDESC2` desc ranges through `crate::ddraw::guest_range` or bounds-checked `Ptr` access, returning `DD::ERR_INVALIDPARAMS` on unusable input. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check` on both files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, and `cargo test -p winapi` (75 tests) pass. The private decrypted PE remains unavailable; next blocker: `gdi32/bitmap.rs` `Bitmap::parse(&ctx.memory[lpbmi..])` (SetDIBitsToDevice header pointer) and the `nls.rs` `ctx.memory[addr..]` readers in `read_string_type_a`/`read_string_type_w`/`read_multibyte`.
- Bitmap/nls bounds iteration: `SetDIBitsToDevice` sliced `ctx.memory[lpbmi..]` before parsing the `BITMAPINFO` header (panic on an out-of-range header pointer), and its `xSrc + w`/`xDest + w`-style checks were u32 adds that a huge start coordinate could wrap past; `StretchBlt` had the same wrap-prone checks and u32 row-offset math. The header read now uses `bytes.get` (returning 0 when absent) and all four rectangle checks widen to u64; row indexing is usize throughout. `Bitmap::stride` computed `width * bit_count` in u32 — a hostile `biWidth` produced a wrapped-tiny stride that let `read_pixels` index a too-short row — it now computes in u64 and saturates to `u32::MAX`, `pixels_len` uses usize math, `read_pixels` guards `x1 > x2` and computes `(y+1)*stride` and all row offsets in usize, and `BITMAPCOREHEADER::stride` no longer multiplies in u16. The `nls.rs` readers `read_string_type_a`/`read_string_type_w`/`read_multibyte` took `&ctx.memory[addr..]`, panicking on a pointer past emulated memory; they now use `bytes.get(addr..)` so `GetStringTypeA/W` and `MultiByteToWideChar` report failure. Added `read_pixels_handles_a_huge_declared_width` and `read_pixels_rejects_x1_past_x2` regressions. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, and `cargo test -p winapi` (77 tests) pass. The private decrypted PE remains unavailable; next blocker: remaining `ctx.memory[..]` slices on internally-derived addresses (resource.rs section reads, ddraw blit row copies) or the next audit cluster.
- LoadLibrary/DLL-path iteration: `LoadLibraryA` sliced the on-disk PE with `&buf[rsrc_off..rsrc_end]`, which panics when a truncated or corrupt DLL declares `PointerToRawData` past the file end (`start > end`), and `rsrc_rva + rsrc_vsize` was an unchecked u32 add that could wrap into a too-small mapping while `loaded_modules` recorded a `resources` range past emulated memory. The raw-data read now uses `buf.get` with a saturated end (failing the load when the range is invalid), and `rsrc_rva + rsrc_vsize` goes through `checked_add`. `GetModuleFileNameA` sliced `ctx.memory[lpFilename..]` after checking only `nSize`, so a buffer pointer near the end of memory panicked; it now validates `copy + 1` bytes via `crate::ddraw::guest_range`. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check win32/winapi/src/kernel32/dll.rs`, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p winapi` (77 tests), and `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2` pass; a 15s headless smoke run reached `Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M` with only the known missing-content warnings and no `out/mm2/missing.txt`. The private decrypted PE remains unavailable; next blocker: continue the audit in `host`/`runtime` (`unsafe_clone` aliasing, `Context::indirect`/`call32_x86` on bad guest addresses) or pick up any new smoke-run finding.
- kernel32 string/time pointer iteration: `read_c_string`, `read_counted_a`, and `read_counted_w` (feeding `lstrlenA`/`lstrcpyA`/`lstrcatA`/`lstrcmpA`/`CompareStringA`/`CompareStringW`), `lstrcpyW`, and `lstrlenW` all sliced `ctx.memory[addr..]` directly, so a guest pointer past emulated memory panicked the host; they now go through `bytes.get` and return their documented failure values. `GetTimeZoneInformation` and `FileTimeToSystemTime` filled `lpTimeZoneInformation`/`lpSystemTime` without a range check — both now `get_mut` the bounded range, returning `TIME_ZONE_ID_INVALID`/`false` on a bad pointer. `GetCurrentDirectoryA` sliced `lpBuffer` after checking only `nBufferLength`; it now validates the `len + 1` range via `guest_range` and returns 0. `GetEnvironmentVariableA`'s `fits` check lacked a null-page guard, so a sub-0x1000 buffer address with a large `nSize` wrote into the null page; it now requires `lpBuffer.addr >= 0x1000`. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check` on the five files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, and `cargo test -p winapi` (77 tests) pass. The private decrypted PE remains unavailable; next blocker: the `user32`/`dinput`/`dmusic` guest-pointer cluster (`misc.rs` `lpchText`, `window.rs` title write, `input.rs` key-name/state writes, `resource.rs` LoadString copies, `dinput.rs` caps/property/device-name buffers, `dmusic.rs` caps fill).
- user32/dinput/dmusic pointer iteration: `GetKeyboardState` wrote 256 bytes at an unchecked `lpKeyState` (wrap or out-of-range panic), `user32/misc.rs::read_bytes0` (the wsprintf `%s`/`fmt` reader) sliced `ctx.memory[addr..]` on an out-of-range pointer, and `GetWindowTextA` lacked the null-page guard next to its length check. `GetKeyboardState` now validates the 256-byte range with `guest_range`, `read_bytes0` returns an empty string on an unreadable address, and `GetWindowTextA` requires `lpString >= 0x1000`. In dinput, `GetCapabilities`/`GetProperty`/`SetProperty`/`GetDeviceInfo` read `dwSize`-style fields through `Memory::read` (panic past end of memory) and added `pdiph + DIPROPDWORD_DWDATA` without an overflow check — all now use `Ptr` reads/writes and `checked_add`, returning `DIERR_INVALIDPARAM`; `GetDeviceData` computed `rgdod + i * cbObjectData` in u32 (a guest stride could wrap the write into a different address) and unwrapped `pdwInOut` — entry addresses are now computed in u64 and each write goes through `Ptr::write`. `dmusic::write_port_caps` read `*caps` unchecked; it now uses `Ptr::read`. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check` on the five files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p winapi` (77 tests), and `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2` pass. The private decrypted PE remains unavailable; next blocker: `user32/window.rs` `Window::flush`'s `ctx.memory[pixels..]` slice on a stale pixel range, `user32/resource.rs`/`gdi32`/`dsound` leftovers, or a fresh audit of `host`/`runtime` internals.
- Window buffer/mixer/ddraw/nls/gdi sweep: `Window::ensure_pixels` computed `width * height * 4` in u32 — two `MAX_WINDOW_DIM` (0x4000) dims wrap to a tiny `try_alloc` while `flush` sliced the full `height * stride` — the alloc now goes through a u64 `try_from` (no buffer for a too-large window) and `flush` uses `bytes.get_mut`. All six `mixer*` exports trusted their struct pointers past the `< 0x1000` check: `mixerGetDevCapsA`/`mixerGetLineInfoA`/`mixerGetLineControlsA`/`mixerGetControlDetailsA`/`mixerSetControlDetails`/`mixerOpen` now validate `pmxcaps`/`pmxl`/`pmxlc`/`pmxcd`/`pamxctrl`/`phmx` via `guest_range` before `Memory::read`/`write`, including the `pamxctrl` control-buffer range. `GetCPInfo` wrote through an unchecked `lpCPInfo` (now `Ptr::write`), `LCMapStringA`/`LCMapStringW` wrote `lpDestStr` after only a `cchDest` size check (now `output_range_fits`), and `WideCharToMultiByte` read `lpDefaultChar` and wrote `lpUsedDefaultChar` unchecked (now `Ptr` access). `IDirectDrawPalette::GetCaps`/`GetEntries`, `IDirectDrawSurface::GetPixelFormat`, `IDirectDraw7::GetCaps` (`lpDDDriverCaps`/`lpDDEmulCaps`), `IDirectDrawSurface7::GetCaps`/`GetClipper`/`GetDC`/`GetPalette`/`GetPixelFormat`, and `IDirect3DVertexBuffer7::GetVertexBufferDesc` all took out-pointers that were only checked for null — each now validates the exact range it writes; `GetEntries` additionally clamps to `min(dwNumEntries, entries - dwBase)` before validating so `entries[dwBase..]` cannot slice past the palette, and `GetSystemPaletteEntries` validates `cEntries * 4` up front with a `wrapping_add` index. `rustfmt --edition 2024 --check` on the touched files, `cargo clippy -p winapi --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p winapi` (77 tests), `cargo build --profile fast -p mm2`, and a 15s headless smoke run all pass — the run reached `Heap MemUsed (Just before GameLoop): 3.0M` with only the known missing-content warnings and no `out/mm2/missing.txt`. The private decrypted PE remains unavailable; next blocker: remaining `Memory::read`/`write` call sites on guest pointers in `ddraw1`/`ddraw7`/`d3d7` (`IDirectDraw::EnumDisplayModes` filter and the surface `SetPalette`/`AddAttachedSurface` handle paths) or the `host`/`runtime` internal audit.
